- 1 - 
訪談速報 
巧新(1563) 
再生鋁與半導體雙引擎推升獲利 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $80 
前次投資建議：2026.05.07 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價52

報告日期：2026.8.11 研究員：陳泂暉

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/8/10 收盤價： 56.5 再生鋁採用率提升以及半導體產品放量，給予投資評等「買
進」 
目前股價 13X 27(F)PER，考量：

(1) 公司 26Q2 營收 19.8 億元，QoQ+5.8%、YoY+15.5%，
營收較上季增加主要是因為下游客戶備貨較為積極；毛利
率 24.3%，QoQ+2.3ppts、YoY+3.5ppts，因再生鋁使用
比率顯著提升與產品優化；稅後淨利 7,119.7 萬元，
QoQ-45.5%、較去年同期虧轉盈，因受到匯率衝擊以及營
業費用受到運輸成本上升影響；稅後 EPS 0.33 元。 
(2) 受新車旺季與高端車款拉貨帶動，預估輪圈出貨 2026 年
逾 60 萬顆（下半年優於上半年），2027 年達 70 萬顆。
公司目標營業利益率回升至 20%；再生鋁採用率達 80%，
經 5個月生產與海運發酵後，將顯著拉升毛利率。 
(3) 屏東二廠預計 27Q2 投產，帶動總熔煉產能達10萬噸 （1/3
自用、2/3外銷代工） 。半導體原生鋁預計 26Q3完成認證，
下半年啟動小量出貨並於 2027 年放量。 
(4) 預估 2027 年營收 85.12 億元 ，YoY+7.7%； 毛利率29.1%，
YoY+4ppts；稅後淨利9.82 億元，YoY+46.7%；稅後EPS 
4.35 元。

巧新目前股價位於 13X27(F)PER，考量到再生鋁使用比重提
高以及半導體產品放量，因此投資評等上調至「買進」，6個
月目標價 80(18.5X2027(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 41.6%。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐國際預估

訪談重點

公司為國內汽車鍛造輪圈大廠 
巧新成立於 1994 年，為國內汽車鍛造輪圈大廠，主要產品包含鍛造鋁圈、
鋁材、其他(主要為鍛造鋁懸吊系統元件 )。公司目前有三座生產基地，分別
是雲林場、屏東廠及德國廠，鍛造輪圈總年產能為90萬件(雲林廠 60 萬件、
屏東廠 30萬件)。

公司客戶包括全球知名車廠，Ferrari(法拉利)、Porsche(保時捷)、Mclaren(麥
拉倫)、Bently(賓利)、Aston Martin、賓士(含 AMG)、BMW(含 Rolls-Royce、
Mini)、Jaguar & Land Rover、Lexus、Lotus及電動卡車製造商 Workhouse
等。

客戶拉貨強勁抵銷匯損衝擊 
公司 26Q2 營收 19.8 億元，QoQ+5.8%、YoY+15.5%，營收較上季增加主
要是因為 下游客戶備貨較為積極 ；毛利率 24.3%， QoQ+2.3ppts、
YoY+3.5ppts，因再生鋁使用比率顯著提升與產品優化 ；稅後淨利 7,119.7
萬元，QoQ-45.5%、較去年同期虧轉盈，因受到匯率衝擊以及營業費用受到
運輸成本上升影響；稅後 EPS 0.33元。

半年輪圈出貨優於上半年，再生鋁採用率提升帶動毛利回升 
展望 26H2，鍛造輪圈受惠於車廠新車與改款車型通常於 10月開始組裝之旺
季需求，因此預計全年輪圈出貨量將超過 60 萬顆，且出貨下半年會優於上
半年。而 2027 年則是持續受惠於高端既有車款的熱門，帶動長期的需求以
及新車與改款的車型帶動下，預估 2027 年全年輪圈出貨量將達到 70萬顆

在毛利與成本表現方面，公司目標將整體營業利益率回升至 20%。由於產品
從場內生產製造（約 2個月）到海運運輸（約 3個月）至客戶端提貨認列存
在約 5 個月的時間差，故成本與毛利優化效益具延後發酵特性。公司目前獲
利優化之核心動能將來自再生鋁材的導入 ，由於再生鋁成本較原生鋁低約
10%至 15%，且截至 5、6月再生鋁佔鍛造輪圈原料採用率來到 80%，預計
將能有效提升毛利率。

- 3 - 
訪談速報 
半導體驗證與產能擴張雙軌併進，帶動長期結構獲利改善 
公司持續拓展鋁材多元應用，目前於屏東設立的首座水平熔煉廠（產能 4 萬
噸）運作穩定，第二座垂直熔煉廠正在建置中，預計 26Q3 完成裝機與參數
調整後，並於 27Q2 投產，總熔煉產能規模規劃將達 10萬噸（約 1/3 自用、
2/3 外銷或代工）。在半導體材料領域，公司持續推進設備腔體與零件之送
樣與驗證作業，原生鋁材料預計 26Q3 完成認證並於下半年啟動少量出貨，
並於 2027 年放量。

再生鋁採用率提升以及半導體產品放量，帶動營收獲利雙增 
預估 26Q3 營收 20.15 億元，QoQ+1.8%、YoY+18.4%；毛利率 25.6%，
QoQ+1.3ppts、YoY+9ppts，受惠於再生鋁採用率上升；稅後淨利 2.39 億元，
QoQ+235.8%、YoY+458.5%；稅後 EPS 1.06 元。

預估 2026年營收 79.02億元，YoY+13.3%；毛利率 25.1%，YoY+3.8ppts；
稅後淨利 6.7億元，YoY+110.4%；稅後 EPS 2.97元。

預估 2027年營收 85.12 億元，YoY+7.7%；毛利率 29.1%，YoY+4ppts；稅
後淨利 9.82 億元，YoY+46.7%；稅後 EPS 4.35 元。

巧新目前股價位於 13X27(F)PER，考量到再生鋁使用比重提高以及半導體產
品放量，因此投資評等上調至 「買進」，6個月目標價 80(18.5X2027(F)PER)，
潛在上漲空間 41.6%。

## Document 010: 1590亞德客 宏遠

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場

1 
1590 亞德客-KY(AIRTAC)

報告日期： 2026/08/11 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 公告 2Q26 稅後 EPS 16.55 元 
亞德客公告 2Q26 營收金額 121.64 億元， YoY+35.87% ，
QoQ+21.48% ，營業利益 42.65 億元， YoY+56.11% ，
QoQ+28.26% ，稅後盈餘 33.11 億元， YoY+62.52% ，
QoQ+23.95% ，稅後 EPS 16.55 元。 1H26 稅後 EPS 29.9 元，優
於去年同期 19.87 元。

二. 預估 3Q26 稅後 EPS 15.33 元 
亞德客 07/2026 營收金額 39.94 億元， YoY+32.87% ，
MoM+2.09% 。預估亞德客 3Q26 營收金額 110.07 億元，
YoY+33.48% ， QoQ-9.51% ，預估營業利益 38.06 億元，
YoY+54.72% ， QoQ-10.75% ，預估稅後盈餘 30.65 億元，
YoY+46.01% ，QoQ-7.41% ，預估稅後 EPS 15.33 元。

三. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 59.00 元 
亞德客 2026 年在對等關稅對中國大陸客戶在產能擴充決策 ， 雖然
會有遞延影響，但整體市場需求己有逐步回溫，加上亞德客持續
配合客戶需求開發新產品提升客戶黏著度 ， 帶動亞德客 2026 年營
運持續成長。預估亞德客 2026 年營收金額 435.00 億元，
YoY+26.71% ，預估稅後盈餘 118.00 億元， YoY+40.47% ，預估
稅後 EPS 59.00 元。

四. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 68.00 元 
亞德客 2027 年在中國政府十五五計畫強調智慧製造及產業升級
伴隨其他刺激政策，有助氣動元件市場需求，加上中國在地化採
購趨勢成型，有助亞德德市占率持續攀升。宏遠預估亞德客 2027
年營收金額 488 億元 ，YoY+12.19% ， 預估稅後盈餘136.00 億元 ，
YoY+15.25% ，預估稅後 EPS 68.00 元。

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 1900 元 
觀察亞德客歷史 ROE 14~31% ，歷史 PER 16~40X 間，  2026 年
ROE 21.7% ，PER 26.3X ，2027 年 ROE 20.3X ，PER 22.8X ，評
價己達歷史相對低檔水準，且 2026 ～2027 年營運成長，故投資
建議買進，目標價 1900 元（ 32.2X 2026 年稅後 EPS ）。

2 
貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
亞德客成立於 1989 年，資本額 20.00 億元，主要以生產氣動元件
電磁閥為主， 1995 年正式進軍中國大陸市場，並逐步將生產線移
住大陸，生產基地包括江蘇寧波、廣東佛山與台灣台南。亞德國
際集團產品主要以特殊規格氣缸為主，生產閥類產品包括電磁
閥、氣動閥、手動閥、手拉閥、機械閥與節流閥等十大類六十多
個系列數百個品種，為專業氣動元件廠商，並自有品牌「 AirTAC 」
行銷全世界。

圖一、公司組織架構

資料來源：亞德客、宏遠投顧整理

二. 2026 年營運概況及展望  
亞德客集團營收來源主要有氣動控制元件 29~33% 、氣動執行元
件 37~41% 、氣源處理元件 10~13% 與其他 16~20% 。下游終端應
用較分散，如電子設備 22~28% 、包裝機械 7~8% 、電池行業
8~14% 、汽車產業 6~7% 、通用機械 6~7% 、機床設備 5~7% 、能
源與照明 6~14% 、其他產業 25~28% 。在營收地區方面，中國大
陸地區比重 94%，非中國大陸地區比重 6%。在產業淡旺季方面，
2Q 旺季、 3Q 次旺季、 4Q 與 1Q 為傳統淡季。

亞德客 01~07/2026 營收金額 261.72 億元， YoY+32.87% 。亞德

3 
客 2026 年營運成長重點包括： 1，在對等關稅對中國大陸客戶在
產能擴充決策，有遞延影響，整體市場 1Q26 需求己有逐步回溫，
加上中國大陸政府仍會視狀況，持續發布政策支持，有助氣動元
件需求回溫。 2，觀察各產業況，電池產業受惠於海外市場放寬中
國廠商限制，預期將帶動電池廠商成長 20~30% ；汽車產業方面，
雖然汽車產業未顯者復甦，亞德客將提升品牌形象，增加汽車產
業滲透率，推估汽車產業成長 10~15% ；電子產業方面，受惠電
子設備客戶推出更多新機型與客戶產品升級，將有助電子產業客
戶成長 10~15% 。3，亞 德 客-KY 不斷開發新產品 、 提升品牌形象，
並依各產業狀況提出相對應銷售策略提高市占率，提升客戶黏著
度。 4，線性滑軌在營運規模逐漸放大，預估貢獻亞德客 2026 年
營收金額 34~38 億元 ，YoY+36~46% 。 綜合上述 ， 預估亞德客2026
年營收金額 435 億元， YoY+26.71% 。

預期 2027 年，亞德客營運主要成長動能包括： 1，中國政府十五
五計畫強調智慧製造及產業升級伴隨其他刺激政策，也有助氣動
元件市場需求。 2，觀察中國官方製造業 PMI 與中國財新製造業
PMI 己由 2026 年初谷底復甦至今皆在 50 以上，有助整體中國製
造業產業景氣持續回溫，且在地化採購趨勢成型，有助亞德德市
占率持續攀升。 3，線軌市場己有復甦跡象，預計 2027 年初稼動
率可達五成，預估貢獻亞德客 2027 年營收金額 48~52 億元，
YoY+37~41%。 綜合上述 ， 預估亞德客2027 年營收金額 488 億元 ，
YoY+12.18% 。

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1 
2344 華邦電(Winbond)

報告日期： 2026/08/07  研 究 員：翁浩軒 (Davy Wong) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 2Q26 毛利率優於預期，EPS 5.4 元 
華邦電 2Q26 營收 598.43 億(QoQ+56.44%、YoY+184.72%)，其中記
憶體事業營收 517 億(QoQ+72.8%、YoY+303.1%)，2Q26 毛利率大
幅跳升至 66.25%，主要因記憶體價格上漲，營業費用提升 28 億主
要因提列員工獎金，稅後淨利 243.17 億，EPS 5.4 元。

二.  2026 記憶體維持上漲格局，預估 EPS 23.68 元 
展望 2026 下半年，預估記憶體求維持強勁下，價格持續上漲，唯
因價格基期已高，加上傳統消費性產品追價力道有限，漲幅不若
上半年，不過預估今年隨著價格上漲，加上製程轉換帶動位元出
貨量增加，營收逐季成長，預估華邦電 2026 年營收 2578.49 億
(YoY+188.4%) ， 稅後淨利 1065.38 億(YoY+2589.04%) ， EPS 23.68 元 。

三.  2027 年記憶體更加緊缺，預估 EPS 43.57 元 
展望 2027 年，AI 帶動之記憶體缺貨狀況延續，加上全球新增產能
有限，緊缺情況更加嚴峻。DDR4 在全球三大廠逐步退出下價格將
維持高檔，NOR Flash 及 SLC NAND 亦因供給端增產有限，價格逐
步回升 ， 其中 SLC Nand 因大廠退出 ， 華邦將成為全球最大供應商 ，
而應用於 AI Server 的高容量 NOR Flash 持續供不應求，預期將推
升價格上行至 2027 年。因應記憶體需求強勁，高雄廠產能將由現
在 15k 擴充至年底的 24k，屆時位元出貨量可望翻倍，同時啟動高
雄 Module B 投資計劃，規劃月產能可達 50k~60k ，將分階段擴充，
預估 2029 年底量產。預估 2027 年營收 4077.20 億(YoY+58.12%)，
稅後淨利 1960.56 億(YoY+84.02%)，EPS 43.57 元

四. 投資建議：買進，目標價 240 元  
華邦電為全球最大 NOR Flash 廠亦是利基型 DRAM 廠，AI 帶動各
式記憶體需求增加，產能仍供不應求下，獲利將逐季成長。公司
針對邊緣 AI 發展之 CUBE 解決方案將於 2027 年開始貢獻 ， 矽電容
亦為新的業務機會，中長期而言 AI、HPC、IoT、自駕車等新產品
新應用將帶動更多記憶體需求。考量到 1) AI 帶動之記憶體供不應
求將延續至 2028 年、2)華邦電今明年營收獲利創高、3)CUBE、矽
電容具成長潛力 ， 參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 5.5 倍本益比 ， 目標價 240
元，建議買進。 
投資評等：買進

     5.5 X 2027 EPS 
參考價位：171.0 元 
     3.92 X 2027 EPS

2 
貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
華邦電子於 1987 年九月由華新麗華集團轉投資成立，為自有品牌
IC 設計及製造廠商，並為全世界第五大 DRAM 供應商，且為國內
少數擁有晶圓廠之積體電路設計公司，同時也是全球利基型記憶
體的主要供應商。2008 年公司將邏輯 IC 事業分割成立新唐科技，
新唐產品線以消費性電子 IC 、電腦 IC 兩大產品線為主，並同時擁
有一座 6 吋晶圓廠。華邦電在 NOR Flash 為全球最大供應商，產
品上從中低容量市場拓展至中高容量市場。2Q26 記憶體產品營收
依應用面而言：消費性電子占 28%、車用&工業用占 28%、通訊電
子占 29%、電腦占 15%。NOR Flash 主要同業為旺宏、兆易創新、
Infineon。

二.2026 年營運概況及展望  
1. 2Q26 毛利率優於預期，EPS 5.4 元 
華邦電 2Q26 營收 598.43 億(QoQ+56.44%、YoY+184.72%)，其中記
憶體事業營收 517 億(QoQ+72.8%、YoY+303.1%)，主要受惠於記憶
體價格價格走揚，DRAM 營收季增 78%，其中價格季增 100%，位
元出貨量季減 10%; Flash 營收季增 65% ，其中價格季增 40% ，位元
出貨量季增 15%，產能利用率維持近 100% 。2Q26 毛利率大幅跳升
至 66.25%，主要因記憶體價格上漲，營業費用提升 28 億主要因
RD 費用增加，加上提列員工獎金，稅後淨利 243.17 億，EPS 5.4
元。市場應用別而言，通訊電子、消費電子、車用&工業用、電腦
等應用營收均較上季季增高雙位數。

2. 2026 下半年記憶體維持上漲格局，預估 2026 年 EPS 23.68 元 
展望 2026 下半年，預估記憶體求維持強勁下，價格持續上漲，唯
因價格基期已高，加上傳統消費性產品追價力道有限，漲幅不若
上半年，不過預估今年隨著價格上漲，加上製程轉換帶動位元出
貨量增加，及新產能貢獻，營收逐季成長，毛利率持續提升，預
估華邦電 2026 年營收 2578.49 億(YoY+188.4%)，毛利率 67.75%，
稅後淨利 1065.38 億(YoY+2589.04%)，EPS 23.68 元。

3. 2027 年記憶體更加緊缺，預估 EPS 43.57 元 
展望 2027 年，AI 帶動之記憶體缺貨狀況延續，加上全球新增產能
有限，緊缺情況更加嚴峻，而儘管終端電子產品需求不一，不過
未來 AI 落地後每單位產品之記憶體搭載量持續成長，使得記憶體
位元出貨提升 ，2027 年公司營運仍正向 。DDR4 在全球三大廠逐步
退出下價格自 2Q25 起漲，且因終端電子產品仍有一定需求，價格

3 
將維持高檔，NOR Flash 及 SLC NAND 亦因供給端增產有限，價格
逐步回升，其中 SLC Nand 因大廠退出，華邦將成為全球最大供應
商，而應用於 AI Server 的高容量 NOR Flash 持續供不應求，預期
將推升價格上行至 2027 年。華邦電製程技術方面，20 奈米 DRAM
產品仍為主要出貨主力，應用於 DDR3、DDR4、LPDDR 產品，16
奈米製程已開發完成，2027 年將有一定比例轉換至 16 奈米；NOR 
Flash 營收穩定成長 ， NAND Flash 24 奈米及 32 奈米 SLC Nand 今年
2Q 貢獻顯著提升。 因應記憶體需求強勁， 公司 Flash 與 DRAM 擴
產計畫進行中，華邦電 2025 年資本支出為台幣 55 億元，2026 年
將提升至 395 億元，其中生產設備資本支出約占 95%，大部分用
於高雄產能及製程提升，少部分用於台中廠製程提升，高雄廠產
能將由現在 15k 擴充至年底的 24k，屆時位元出貨量可望翻倍，同
時啟動高雄 Module B 投資計劃，規劃月產能可達 50k~ 60k，將分
階段擴充，預估 2027 年 1 月動工，2029 年 1 月裝機，2029 年底量
產。2027 年預估 DRAM 、NOR Flash 及 SLC NAND 價格持續上漲帶
動營收獲利成長，預估 2027 年營收 4077.20 億(YoY+58.12%)，毛
利率 74.52%，稅後淨利 1960.56 億(YoY+84.02%)，EPS 43.57 元。

4. CUBE 及矽電容為新成長動能 
AI 時代來臨，華邦電長期專注於利基型 DRAM ，針對 AI 將主攻邊
緣運算市場，邊緣運算之記憶體容量不需像 AI Server 那麼高，為
華邦電可著墨之區塊，因此華邦電提供 CUBE(Customized Ultra 
Bandwidth Element 客製化超高頻寬記憶體元件)解決方案，CUBE
具備超低功耗和超高性能、小尺寸、高成本效益，從而大幅提升
系統能力、回應時間和能源效率，以滿足邊緣運算平臺上快速增
長的 AI 應用需求。CUBE 優點在於 1)SoC 推疊在上面，散熱效果
較好、2) 可在 CUBE Die 上做穿孔(TSV)，降低成本、3) 3D 推疊
適合較小的終端裝置。目前華邦電與多家客戶開發 CUBE 方案，
預估 2026 年底開始貢獻，2027 年放量。另外因應 AI 晶片功耗劇
增、瞬間電流波動，以及先進封裝的空間限制，矽電容具有極低
的等效串聯電感與寄生電感，能在極短時間內補償電流，維持電
源完整性，避免電壓驟降，也彌補傳統 MLCC 的不足，華邦電因
客戶需求提供矽電容 foundry 代工，目前產能已滿載，未來 CUBE
及矽電容均為新成長動能。

華邦電 2Q26 營收 598.43 億(QoQ+56.44%、YoY+184.72%)，其中記
憶體事業營收 517 億(QoQ+72.8%、YoY+303.1%)。2Q26 毛利率大

產品組合

股價相對大盤走勢

華邦電 (2344 TT)  Buy

記憶體報價續漲，建議 Buy。  
投資建議 ：AI 的發展改變產業追求性價比與記憶體單價的傳統 ，
轉而重視效能、算力及大容量。記憶體市場供需出現結構性改
變。進入 3Q26 DRAM 與 Flash 的價格仍維持上行趨勢，雖然價
格上漲趨勢較緩，但毛利率仍持續向上。預估華邦電 2026 年稅
後 EPS 為 24.85 元。除 AI 伺服器需求外，交換器及 Edge AI 應
用亦帶動 NOR Flash 與 SLC NAND 需求持續成長，華邦電憑藉
NOR Flash 全球市占率第一及 SLC NAND 擴產，可望持續受惠。
此外，公司矽電容目前產能接近滿載，受惠 AI 先進封裝需求快
速成長，將成為新的獲利來源。過去 3~4 個月產業雖歷經多項
利空干擾，但 DRAM 及 NAND 報價持續走揚，顯示供需結構未
見轉弱，對華邦電建議 Buy ，目標價 200 元 (PER8X) 。  
華邦電 2Q26 稅後 EPS 5.40 元：受惠記憶體報價上漲， 2Q26 華
邦電合併營收 598.43 億元， QoQ+56.44% 。 CMS( 原  Specialty 
DRAM 改名客製化記憶體方案 )占營收比重  53% 、Flash 占營收
比重  33% 、 Logic 占  13% ，其中  CMS 營收 QoQ+78.21% ，成
長最明顯。 2Q26 合併毛利率為  66.25% ，增加約  12.88 個百分
點。2Q26 記憶體的毛利率從  1Q26 的  56.6% 提升至  70.3% ，
1Q26 稅後 EPS 5.40 元。  
資本支出 395 億元 ：受設備交機時程影響，預估 2026 年資本支
出下修為 395 億元， YoY+618.18% 。部分原訂於 2026 年底交貨
的設備將遞延跨年至 2027 年付款，但整體擴產進度不僅如期，
甚至超前，來自製程的轉換。目前台中廠  DRAM 月產能約  
12K 。配合高雄廠  16nm 新產能於 2027 年開出，公司規劃於  
2027 年將台中廠  DRAM 月產能降至約  7K 。高雄廠  Module 
A 目前月產能約  15K ，預計在  2026 年底前提升至 24K 並開
始新增產能投片。 16nm 月產能目前約  2K 。華邦電已正式啟動
高雄廠  Module B 的全新階段擴產計畫，目標 2029 年年中開始
裝機，並於  2029 年底前進入量產。

2 群益投顧 
  華邦電 ESG ：  
華邦電堅持所有經營管理活動必須兼顧企業社會責任。經由加強員工教育
訓練及公司內控制度，以確保企業道德及政府法令之遵循。同時，持續不斷
地追求品質管理和環境保護之改善，勠力於社會公益，維持與利害關係人間
之良好溝通。期待所有事業夥伴支持並遵循企業社會責任之要求。

華邦電 2Q26 營收 598.43 億元， QoQ+56.44% ，稅後淨利 243.17 億元，
QoQ+140.42% ，稅後 EPS 5.40 元：  
受惠記憶體報價上漲， 2Q26 華邦電合併營收 598.43 億元， QoQ+56.44% 。
CMS( 原  Specialty DRAM 改名客製化記憶體方案 )占營收比重  53% 、 Flash
占營收比重  33% 、 Logic 占  13% ，其中  CMS 營收 QoQ+78.21% ，成長最
明顯。 2Q26 華邦電記憶體營收為  517.00 億元， QoQ+72.78% ， YoY 
+303.15% 。Flash 和  CMS 各占記憶體營收  39% 和  61% 。華邦電 2Q26 合
併毛利率為  66.25% ，較 1Q26 的 53.37% 增加約  12.88 個百分點。 2Q26 
記憶體的毛利率從  1Q26 的  56.6% 顯著提升至  70.3% ，產能利用率維持
100% 的滿載狀態，邏輯 (新唐 )毛利率則為  38.0% 。

### Page 3

個股報告

3 群益投顧 
營業費用方面， 2Q26 合併營益率為  48.43% ，稅後獲利為  243.17 億元，
QoQ +140.42% ，稅後 EPS 5.40 元。每股淨值從  1Q26 的  25.92 元增加至
36.67 元。    
若單獨以記憶體來看， 2Q26 記憶體產品營收比重方面， Flash 產品線占記
憶體營收比重  39% ，營收 QoQ +65% 、YoY+176% ，位元出貨量 (bit shipment)
QoQ+14~+16% 、YoY+14~+16% ，在 NOR 與 SLC NAND 供給偏緊的態勢下，
成功推升 ASP (US$) QoQ+41~+43% 、 YoY>+100% 。 32nm SLC NAND Flash
占  Flash 營收比重為  25% ，24nm 占比達  4% ，製程持續轉進； 45nm Flash 
占比維持  6% 。高容量產品線整體出貨占比持續提升，反映 NOR 與 SLC 
NAND 需求暢旺，自 2Q26 起， 24nm SLC NAND 將逐步帶來顯著營收貢
獻。。  
CMS 占記憶體營收 61%，營收  QoQ +78% 、YoY +467% ，位元出貨量  QoQ 
-11~-13% 、YoY+21~+23% ，資料中心、 Edge AI 及智慧家庭等應用帶動結構
性供給缺口，使得 ASP (US$) QoQ 約 100% 、 YoY+>300% 。 2Q26 的  20nm 
占營收比重達  71% ，較  1Q26 的  68% 提升，主要受惠於 AI 強勁需求，
DDR4/LPDDR4 產品線的顯著成長與產品組合優化， ≦25nm 營收貢獻占比
達 93% 。  
從產品應用比重來看 ， 消費性電子  (Consumer) 占營收比重 28%，QoQ +64% ；
通訊電子  (Communication) 占營收比重  29% ， QoQ+82% ，受惠於網通與
WiFi 應用需求成長。電腦相關  (Computer) 占  15% ，QoQ+89% ，筆電需求
保持健康；車用及工業用相關  (Car & Industrial) 占營收比重  28% ， QoQ 
+54% ，車用需求穩定增加，使用容量增加，而工業和網路設備的  SLC 晶
片 design-in 數量也正在增加。相關應用對高密度記憶體與  Secure Flash 的
需求持續提升。

### Page 4

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 7 日

4 群益投顧 
395 億元， YoY+618.18% ，其中生產設備資本支出約占 95%( 特別是晶圓廠
設備 )，以加速增產。主要因產能擴充及設備交貨延遲，部分設備交貨延至
2027 年。儘管目前許多機器設備交期拉長，部分原訂於 2026 年底交貨的設
備將遞延跨年至 2027 年付款，但整體擴產進度不僅如期，甚至超前，來自
製程的轉換。 1H26 已實際支出新台幣  75 億元。  
目前台中廠  DRAM 月產能約  12K 。配合高雄廠  16nm 新產能於  2027 年
開出，公司規劃於  2027 年將台中廠  DRAM 月產能降至約 7K。  
高雄廠  Module A 目前月產能約 15K ，預計在  2026 年底前提升至 24K 並
開始新增產能投片。 16nm 月產能目前約  2K ，現階段良率接近  80% ，目標
在  2026 年底前提升至接近 90% 。長期理想目標是將  16nm 產能拉高至
16K ，逐步取代舊製程。受惠於 16nm 產能的開出，預估  2027 年高雄廠的
位元產出將接近倍增。  
高雄廠  Module A 達到  24K 產能後，空間已塞滿，因此華邦電已正式啟動
高雄廠  Module B 的全新階段擴產計畫，目標在  01/2027 正式動工，預計
2029 年年中開始裝機，並於  2029 年底前進入量產。 Module B 的潔淨室
面積約  30,000 平方公尺，約為現有  Module A 的兩倍。完整裝滿後可容納
約  50K 至  60K 的月產能，但公司強調會配合客戶的預測與  LTA( 長期合
約 )採分階段裝機，不會一次裝滿。  
規劃生產  Standard DRAM 、 CUBE DRAM( 客製化記憶體 )、 Wafer-on-
Wafer( 晶圓堆疊 )及矽電容 (Si-Cap) 。 Module B 已預留 14nm 、 12nm 及
EUV( 極紫外光 )的製程空間。其中  14nm 將同時開發非  EUV 及  EUV 版
本， EUV 版為後續  12nm 做準備。因  EUV 設備交期長達  3 至  3.5 年，
公司必須提前向設備廠規劃。已有客戶開始提前洽談  2029 至  2030 年
Module B 的產能與  LTA 。  
公司先前投入約新台幣  40 億元建立額外的矽電容產線，目前產能已全數
售罄。矽電容採用獨立產線配置，因此擴產與裝機完全不會排擠到  16nm
DRAM 的產能與設備規劃。由於產能供不應求，公司目前正積極在高雄廠
尋找新的空間以利後續擴產。值得注意的是，現階段規劃的  395 億元資本
支出尚未包含高雄廠新廠  Module B 的建置與裝機費用，未來的  Module B 
裝機將會是另一筆巨大的投資金額。

5 群益投顧 
圖一：華邦電記憶體資本支出

資料來源：華邦電，群益投顧預估整理 
華邦電在矽電容發展較預期佳：  
華邦電的矽電容起初並非獨立規劃的生意，而是華邦電在開發客製化記憶
體 (Cube) 時，為解決電壓驟降 (voltage drop) 而提供給客戶的保護電路。由於
產品規格極佳，部分非客製化記憶體的客戶進而主動要求試作，華邦電順勢
將其發展成一項獨立產品。  
隨著 AI、先進封裝 (如 CoWoS 等 )及細微穩壓 (1.2 伏、 0.9 伏以下 )的需求爆
發，矽電容因具備極小、極薄、耐高溫且電容值精準的特性，迅速成為解決
封裝雜訊與穩壓的最佳方案。  
華邦電矽電容的規格：目前正進行小量產的規格為  3,300 nF/mm ²，而最新
送樣的規格已達到  5,400 nF/mm ²。台積電的量產規格為 2,500 nF/mm ²(主
要用於其  interposer 上供客戶使用 )，其他同業則約在  1,500 nF/mm ²。華 邦
電在此領域具備顯著的領先優勢，並預期很快會成為全球最大的供應商。  
為避免矽電容生產擠壓到利潤更好的 DRAM 產能，華邦電特別為矽電容開
闢獨立的生產專線。公司先前已針對此專線投資約新台幣  40 億元，但產能
一開出便售光，目前高雄廠的矽電容產能已呈現滿載狀態，公司正積極尋找
新的空間進行擴產 。 由於矽電容的前景看好，目前已有數家原先製造  MLCC 
的廠商轉而進入此領域，並成為華邦電該項服務的客戶。  
相較於生產需要耗時 170 天的  DRAM ，矽電容的生產 cycle time 只需要 30
天。在相同的生產時間內， DRAM 跑 1 個生產 cycle ，矽電容則可以跑 5 個
cycle ，這大幅提升設備的周轉率。  
華邦電原本預期矽電容在  2027 年時能占  DRAM 營收的  10% 。然而由於
DRAM 本身的營收分母在近期成長得太大 ， 要達到  10% 的占比變得困難 ，
目前評估能維持  5% 營收占比。不過，矽電容發展的絕對總金額與公司原
先計畫的預期差不多，公司依舊看好其長期的成長動能。  
價格維持高檔，預估華邦電 2026 年營收 2,615.23 億元， YoY+192.51% ，稅
後獲利 1,118.29 億元， YoY+2,722.59% ，稅後 EPS 24.85 元：

個股報告

6 群益投顧 
華邦電在 AI 邊緣運算方面，提供 CUBE(Customized Ultra Bandwidth
Element) 方案，並參加 UCIe 聯盟，華邦電自主開發高頻寬 CUBE 記憶體，
適用於邊緣 AI 應用， AI、 HPC 領域的  3D TSV DRAM ，華邦電目前 CUBE
在概念驗證 (POC) 階段，先在台中廠試做中，先以 25Snm 開發，後續會以
20nm/16nm 製程生產，可提供每顆晶片 256Mb~16Gb 容量， 2026 年有 16nm
製程， 2027 年可望貢獻營收， Hybrid bump WoW 可以推到 8 顆，再去切，
是以 good die base 出貨。華邦電之前與聯電 (2303 TT) 等合作夥伴成立晶圓
對晶圓 (wafer-to-wafer, W2W) 3D IC 專案，協助客戶加速 3D 封裝產品生
產。此項合作案聯電負責 CMOS 晶圓製造和晶圓對晶圓混合封裝技術；華
邦電導入客製化超高頻寬元件  (Customized Ultra- Bandwidth Elements, 
CUBE) 架構，用於邊緣運算 AI 設備，實現在各種平台和介面上的無縫部署，
但目前尚未出貨。  
管理層明確定位其角色更接近  ASIC ，配合客戶  XPU 進行  3D 封裝，使
華邦電與大宗  DRAM 商品市場的供給溢出效應脫鉤 。 一旦  CUBE 2027 年
起放量，公司的訂價邏輯將不再跟隨  DRAM 現貨市場起伏，而是依附在客
戶的  AI 晶片投資週期之上，這是一種更具黏性、更高毛利的客戶關係結
構。  
Code Storage Flash 方面， NOR Flash 供給在高容量終端需求帶動下持續偏
緊，推升價格於 1H26 起維持有利的上行趨勢。 SLC NAND 供給短缺態勢
亦升溫，支撐價格在  2026 年後延續上漲動能。主要成長來自  伺服器、車
用、 AI 相關應用。在消費端，隨  TWS 等應用功能複雜化，具備較小尺寸
優勢的  45nm Flash 持續推動  Design-in 。新產品自  2Q26 起， 24nm SLC 
NAND 將逐步帶來顯著的營收貢獻。    
Customized Memory Solution (CMS) 方面， DDR4 / LPDDR4 存在結構性供
給缺口，預期將延續至  2028 年以後，短期供給改善空間有限。在供給吃緊
及  AI 需求 (資料中心、 Edge AI 等 )挹注下， 2Q26 價格上漲空間明顯，對
2H26 至  2027 年維持正向展望。眾多客戶的系統單晶片 (SoC) 仍採用  22nm 
或  14nm 製程，短期內難以支援  DDR5 ，使  DDR4 需求得以延續  。隨著
三大原廠將製程升級至  14nm 以下，受限於  ECC 補償機制的技術差異，
已無法回頭量產  DDR4 。隨著大廠逐步淡出供給，現有產能已不足以滿足市
場長期的結構性需求，這正驅動  DDR4 與  DDR3 的合約價持續攀升，且
漲勢預期將一路延續至  2026 年底  。在應用端，智慧家庭、無人機、 M2M 
及網通設備在  2026 年的需求極為看好，電視相關應用需求亦維持健康。特
別是  20nm 製程在  2025 年呈現指數型成長，營收占比已達  55% ，這股成
長動能預計將持續貫穿整個  2026 年。  
過去簽  LTA 的主要是華邦電的直接客戶 (EC1) ，但 1Q26 管理層明確指出，
連二、三線終端廠商 (EC2、EC3) 乃至整車廠原廠，都開始繞過中間層直接找
上華邦電要求簽署長達三年的合約，部分  DDR4 客戶甚至提出 7-10 年的
超長期鎖貨需求。這種行為在供應鏈中屬於高度罕見，清楚說明市場對
DDR4 中長期供應的焦慮已從系統廠蔓延至整個生態鏈末端。對華邦電而
言， LTA 需求的激增提升營收能見度。  
Samsung 、 SK Hynix 、 Micron 陸續退出  2D NAND(SLC ／ MLC) 市場，將資
源集中於  3D NAND 高容量產品。這對華邦電而言，競爭者主動騰出的利
基空間，使其在  SLC NAND 的定價主導權顯著提升。 DRAM 端亦是相同

### Page 7

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 7 日

7 群益投顧 
邏輯：三大廠將  DDR4 產能陸續轉向  DDR5 及更先進製程，導致  DDR4 
供應持續吃緊，華邦電的中小容量  DDR4 產品 (4Gb 、 8Gb 、 16Gb) 在
Enterprise SSD Cache 領域幾乎面臨無競爭對手局面。  
華邦電表示，進入 3Q26 ， DRAM 與  Flash 的價格仍維持上行趨勢，但管
理階層表示不預期 ASP 會再次出現單季 100% 的翻倍增長。 CMS 的價格將
大致跟隨市場報價進行調整。由於全球主要供應商逐步淡出  2D NAND 及
低容量  eMMC( 迫使約 15% – 20% 的低容量需求轉向  SLC NAND) ，在供給
持續緊縮且客戶強勁的設計導入 (design-ins) 支持下 ， 價格漲勢預期將穩定延
續至  2H26 。 NOR Flash 受惠於 AI 伺服器，單台新世代機架估計需使用約
500 – 600 顆  NOR Flash 、車用等強勁需求，高容量  NOR Flash 市場持續
供不應求，將推升價格上行趨勢至  2026 年以後。由於華邦電在  NOR Flash 
市場穩居全球第一市占率 (約  23%) ，公司表示會兼顧市場份額與客戶關係，
不會主動帶頭大幅漲價。  
華邦電基本不參與波動較大的現貨市場，主要採行季度合約報價 (quarterly 
base) 。所有產品全數以合約價成交、不走現貨市場，顯示公司對後續定價走
勢具有相當程度的掌控力。由於管理階層預期 2027 年的記憶體供應將會比
2026 年更加吃緊。公司在滿足客戶基本合約需求後，選擇在 2Q26 適度調
控與減少出貨量，將部分產品留到後續再出貨，以因應未來幾年產能吃緊的
市況並發揮最大商業價值。預估華邦電 2026 年稅後 EPS 24.85 元。

無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

買進 $252 
前次投資建議：2026.07.16 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價: $240

報告日期：2026.08.10 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ AI 導線架動能強勁，均熱片新案持續發酵，2026 年將重返榮耀，EPS 6.5 元。 
➢ AI 產品全面放量，均熱片加入貢獻，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利再上層樓，EPS 8.4 元，
考量到今年獲利上修且未來長期營運動能強勁，上調投資評等為「買進」 ，目標價252 元(30X 
27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 48.2%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/8/7 收盤價： 170.00 ➢ 展望 2026 年，預期AI 相關營收將倍數增長，車用受
惠庫存調整結束與新車款推出，均熱片預計認證後進度
可望加速。受惠 26H1 漲價帶動，稼動率維持高檔，
同時產品組合轉佳下，將有助於本業持續改善。綜上所
述，考量 H1 財報優於預期，展望更趨正向，兆豐 投
顧 上 修 順 德 2026 年營收  137.1 億 元 ，YoY 
+33.8%，毛利率 20.3%，年增 7ppt，稅後淨利 12.5 
億元，YoY +313%，EPS 6.5 元。

+-400V/800V HVDC 架構滲透率提升帶動AI導線架營

可做到約 30μm 的薄型化水準，優於同業約 50–60

個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

順德 2025年跨入 AI server領域，2026年散熱領域正式發酵 
順德(2351)成立於 1967年，以辦公文具起家，1983 年跨入半導體導線架，
並於 2010 年與日本三菱伸銅(mitsubishi-shindoh)技術合作，取得導線架原
料銅合金製程設備及技術授權，目前產品涵蓋 IC 導線架、功率/分離式元件
導線架、LED 導線架、音圈馬達彈片，終端應用遍及車用、工業與消費性電
子。順德目前在全球導線架市佔排名前五，但在 Power IC 導線架則排名第
一。順德過往多以車用產品為主，不過隨著資料中心電源架構的改變，順德
與英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管理經驗移轉至資料中心，順德成為全台唯一 AI 
server 導線架主力供應商，並於 2025 年開始出高功率導線架與穩壓器。而
順德自 2024 年布局均熱片跨足散領域，並於 2026 年開始出 CPO Switch產
品，而多家 CSP 之 CPU與 GPU 也有望於本年度驗證後開始出貨。

個股報告 
車用持續復甦與 AI 導線架需求強勁，26Q2 營收與毛利率皆優預期，惟費
用增，EPS 1.6 元 
順德(2351)公告 26Q2 財報：營收 35.5 億元，QoQ +26.4%/YoY +36.6%，，
優於預期，主要受惠車用與消費性需求同步回溫、AI 導線架需求高速增長，
全產品線均季增超過 20%；同時在報價調漲與稼動率提升、且生產效率提升
下，毛利率 20.8%，季增1.7ppt，創近年新高，也優於研究部預估之 18.5%，
不過因人事獎金與一次性 RBA 移工費用(約 5,000 萬元)提列，使費用季增
21%至 3.2 億元，故稅後淨利 3 億元，QoQ +36.8%/YoY 虧為盈，EPS 1.6
元，略低於前次上修後之 1.7 元。

車用展望佳、AI 放量加速，上修獲利預估，26Q3 EPS 1.8 元 
順德(2351)公告 7 月營收：營收 13.3 億元，MoM +9.4%/YoY +60.8% ，再
創單月新高，展望 26Q3，兆豐投顧上修營收至 36.5 億元，QoQ +3%/YoY 
+43.6%，主要動能來自車用需求全面復甦與  AI 導線架供不應求，車用方
面：除歐美車廠需求延續外，陸系新品也將推出，預期車用成長動能將延續，
此外 AI 導線於 Q2 有近 30%季增長，預期在新產能開出下，營收將逐月創高，
而在產品組合優化與稼動率提升下，預估毛利率維持 20%高檔水準，同步上
修稅後淨利 3.5 億元，QoQ +15.4% /YoY + 301%，EPS 1.8 元(原估 1.75
元)。

AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求明顯回溫，均熱片新案持續發酵，2026 年將
重返榮耀，EPS 6.5 元 
展望 2026 年，順德最大成長動能來自 AI 產品線，隨著伺服器電源架構改
變，PSU 瓦數持續提升，高功率導線架需求將被徹底激發，從高壓電網、
SST(固態變壓器)、Power Stage、48V bus converter、800V HVDC 與 BBU 
皆須用到高功率導線架，而順德自 2024 年攜手英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管
理經驗移轉至資料中心 ，使順德成為全球  AI server 導線架主力供應商之
一，目前有超過 40 支產品，同時在擴產效益帶動下，預期 2026 年 AI 相關
營收將倍數增長；在車用方面，受惠庫存調整結束，同時電動車需求激增與
新車款推出，預期全年呈雙位數增長；在均熱片方面，公司耕耘近兩年，近
期通過 2 CSP 客戶廠房稽核，預計認證後進度可望加速，更大尺寸產品用於 
CPU/GPU 將陸續驗證出貨，惟現階段出貨量較小且產能有限。毛利率方面：
受惠 26H1 漲價帶動，稼動率維持高檔，同時產品組合轉佳下，將有助於本
業持續改善。綜上所述，考量 H1 財報優於預期，展望更趨正向，兆豐投顧
上修順德 2026 年營收 137.1 億元，YoY +3 3.8%，毛利率 20.3%，年增 
7ppt，稅後淨利 12.5 億元，YoY +313%，EPS 6.5 元。

個股聚焦

2026/8/7

股東權益 (NT$百萬元) 50,456

投資建議 買進

公司簡介 
台光電為國內專業銅箔基板廠商 ， 主要產品
包含銅箔基板 、 黏合片(PP)以及多層壓合基
板，2Q26 終端應用為行動裝置(10-15%)、
基礎設施(75-80%)、以及汽車 /工業/其他
(5-10%)，過去公司主要強項在無鹵素材料
以及手機 PP 配方，由於 PP 毛利率高於平
均且隨著 iPhone 滲透率提升帶動公司營
運逐年成長，公司亦積極轉型往基地台、
Server 等網通以及車用相關應用發展，並
成為通吃 GPU 及 ASIC AI server CCL供應
商。

主要客戶：華通、臻鼎-KY、欣興、健鼎等 
主要競爭對手： 聯茂、台燿、 Doosan、
Panasonic

廖貫捷 kuanchieh.liao@sinopac.com 
本次報告：座談會

台光電 (2383 TT) 
高速 CCL 王者，2026-2028 年獲利高速成長

永豐觀點 
台光電通吃 ASIC AI Server 訂單，高階料號市占率 38.6%全球第一，2Q26 毛利
率創高令人驚艷，中線在 800G、1.6T Switch 產品週期推升下 2026 年獲利持續
創高，長線 M9 料號貢獻，2026-2028 年獲利高速成長。 
 投資評價與建議 
台光電在 AI Server 接單無虞下產能吃緊，2Q26 毛利率創高來到 33.9%優於預
期，，長線而言M9 於 2H26 貢獻營運規格升級趨勢不變，公司亦規劃 2028 年底
月產能擴充至 1110 萬張較 2026 年增加 80.4%，營運價量齊揚態勢不變，研究部
上調 2026-2028 年稅後 EPS 至 115.37、211.35 以及 339.22 元，投資建議維持
買進，目標價上調至 6340 元(30X2027 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
台光電企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 B+等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

台光電為國內專業銅箔基板廠商，主要產品包含銅箔基板、黏合片(PP)以及多層壓
合基板，2Q26 終端應用為行動裝置(10-15%)、基礎設施(75-80%)、以及汽車/工
業/其他(5-10%)，過去公司主要強項在無鹵素材料以及手機 PP 配方，由於 PP 毛
利率高於平均且隨著 iPhone 滲透率提升帶動公司營運逐年成長 ， 公司亦積極轉型
往基地台、Server 等網通以及車用相關應用發展，並成為通吃 GPU 及 ASIC AI 
server CCL 供應商。公司下游客戶為華通、臻鼎-KY、欣興以及健鼎等終端客戶包
含 Samsung、APPLE、華為、Cisco，主要競爭對手為聯茂、台燿、Doosan 以及
Panasonic。 
2Q26 毛利率 33.9%創高，本業表現令人驚豔：台光電 2Q26 稅後 EPS 27.55 元，
優於研究部原先預估的 22.15 元水準，其中毛利率較 1Q26 增加 4.5 個百分點來
到 33.9%創高並優於預期 3.1 個百分點，主要原因來自稼動率維持滿載、產品組合
轉佳以及漲價效應，其中受惠 AI server 需求暢旺，基礎設施占比來到 75-85%，
帶動 M7 以上高階料號佔比從 1Q26 的 55-60%進一步提升至 60-65%， 即便6 月
第二次漲價未完全反映，本業表現仍令人驚艷。 
AI 需求撐腰， 3Q26 營收 QoQ+21.1% ： 台光電 7 月營收 192.07 億元
(+8.3%MoM，+129.4%YoY)整體 3Q26 營收達成率 33.6%符合預期，研究部認
為 3Q26 除了 AI server 需求推動 M7 以上高階料號占比提升至 65-70%外，6 月
份 漲 價 效 應 亦 可 望 完 整 季 度 貢 獻 ， 預 估 營 收572.44 億元 (+21.1%QoQ ，
+127.7%YoY)，毛利率在稼動率滿載及產品組合轉佳下提升至 35.5%，稅後淨利
128.83 億元(+30.54%QoQ，+254.3%YoY)，單季稅後 EPS 35.96 元(原估 25.37
元)。 
2H26 仍具漲價空間，上調 2026 年稅後 EPS 至 115.37 元：台光電基礎設施產品
線毛利率優於公司平均水準，其中 AI server 占 55-60%、General server 占 15-
20%、Switch 占 15-20%、LEO 占 15-20%，整體櫃間傳輸以及 Agentic AI 帶動
的 General server 等泛 AI 需求達 75-80%，展望 2026 年不論 GPU 及 ASIC 陣營
相關需求不墜，稼動率持續維持滿載，另一方面 M7 以上 CCL 在供給吃緊下擴產
能見度已達 2028 年，2H26 仍具漲價空間，研究處上調台光電 2026 年營收至
1942.72 億元 (+106.1%YoY) ，毛利率 34.3% ， 稅 後 淨 利413.36 億元
(+182.2%YoY)，稅後 EPS 115.37 元(原估 84.89 元)。

營運現況與分析 
表一：台光電 2Q26 及 2026 年獲利預估調整 
百萬元 2Q26(新估) 2Q26(原估) 差異(%) 2026(新估) 2026(原估) 差異(%)

M9 於 2H26 問世 ，CCL 料號升級趨勢不變： 台光電於2020 年來積極切入 Server、
Switch 以及 AI Server 等高速傳輸領域持續侵蝕同業聯茂(6213 TT) 和台燿(6274 
TT)於 Server 及 Switch 市占，2023 年並成為美系 AI Server 首輪供應商，帶動相
關基礎設施產線於2Q26 達 75-80%，毛利率超越 Handheld 成為全公司最佳，長
線而言隨著 ASIC AI Server 以及 800G、1.6T Switch 產品週期推升，M8 比重將於
2025 年攀升，而M9 亦將於 2H26 問世並占比低個位數，公司產品組合轉佳趨勢不
變。 
AI server 需求強勁，擴產規畫達 2028 年：隨著 AI Server 於高速傳輸需求殷切，
不論櫃內連結的 Switch Tray，或者機櫃間連接的乙太網路規格皆持續升級，另外
Agentic AI 亦帶動一般 server 快速成長，以 AMD 預期 2030 相關 CPU TAM 達
2000 以美元以上計算 CAGR 達 40-45%，且 Oak Stream 新平台下料號升級至
M7，整體高階料號供給吃緊，以目前土建 1.-1.5 年，設備交期 0.5-1.0 年計算，
新產能最快開出時點落於 2028 年，預計 4Q26 大園擴增 30 萬張新產能總計月產
能達 615 萬張(+17.1%YoY)，2027 年在中山、昆山以及檳城產能擴充下年底月產
能達 930 萬張(+51.2%YoY)，2028 年年底更上看 1110 萬張(+19.4%YoY)，考量
規格升級以及高階料號潛在供給吃緊後台光電 2027-2028 年獲利成長具上調空
間 ， 預估稅後EPS 分別上調至 211.35 元(原估 159.73 元)及 339.22 元(原估 247.13
元)。

Chroma ATE (2360.TW)
06 August 2026 Citi Research
5
Chroma ATE
Valuation
Our target price of NT$2,500 is based on 40x 2027E P/E, at close to 1x PEG. Chroma shares have re-rated above its historical
range as its growth outlook is now strongly tied to AI infrastructure build out. We estimate 60%+ of its revenue are now AI-
related, with strong growth across product segments including power testers, SLT, metrology, and photonics. Chroma has a
commanding market share in most of these segments and well positioned to capitalized on the global AI expansion.

Risks
Downside risks to our target price include: 1) softer AI/CPO growth expectation due to changes in capex outlook or production
bottleneck; 2) fewer SLT needed as a result of shortened cycle time as GPU yield rate improves; 3) disappointing metrology
shipment due to changes at ramp-up schedule; 4) testing process change leading to loss in market share; 5) FX risks (50-60%
of Chroma’s sales are in US$, 10-20% in Rmb).
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Bull/Bear: WT Micro (3036.TW)

### Page 6

WT Micro (3036.TW)
06 August 2026 Citi Research
6
WT Micro
Company description
Founded in 1993, WT Micro focuses on semiconductor-related component
agency sales. Currently, it ranks among the top four IC distributors globally
and holds a market share of around 12%. Headquartered in Taiwan, WT Micro
has an extensive marketing and sales channel with nearly 50 regional offices
across China, Korea, Singapore, India, Thailand, Malaysia, and Vietnam. WT

Micro currently serves as the distribution partner to >25,000 customers for
over 80 global leading semiconductor partners. As of 2023, 28% revenues
came from mobile phone, 22% from datacenter, 14% from communication,
10% from industrial, 10% from PC, 9% from consumer and 8% from
automotive application
Investment strategy
We rate WT Micro as Buy. From a top-down view, we expect the IC
distribution industry to be aided by semi upcycle on AI in 2026. We also see

global supply chain relocation favoring WT Micro going forward given its more
diversified global footprints. For its subsidiary, Future Electronics, which WT
Micro acquired in 2024, we are seeing the merger bearing fruit given ongoing
recovery in auto/industrial business for US/European customers with higher
GM profile. Furthermore, we believe there is significant further potential from
synergies, primarily through cross-selling to each company's customer base

and securing new distribution rights in the U.S. and Europe.
Valuation
We use DCF methodology to derive our target price of NT$365. Key
assumptions of our DCF model include a risk-free rate of 4.1% (10-year
government bond rate), a market risk premium of 7%, and an equity beta of
0.9 (1-year weekly share price), which derives a WACC of 8.6%. Our model
assumes 1% long-term revenue growth and gradual expanding of EBIT margin
from 1.9% in 2028 to 2.3% in the long term. Our target price is equivalent to

13x/11x 2026/27E EPS and 3.3x/2.5x BVPS, which is in line with its peers'
valuation and we believe is justified by AI trend, upsides from its subsidiary,
Future Electronics.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent WT Micro’s share price from reaching
our target price are: 1) weaker-than-expected end market demand; 2) lower-
than-expected synergy from the acquisition of Future; 3) taking longer time to
acquire new distribution rights or 4) aggressive funding for its working capital

via higher leverage ratio (more interest expense) or GDR issuance (earnings
dilution).

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and

### Page 9

9
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.

Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

## Document 088: 260806_daiwa_novatek

Source PDF: all report pdf/260806_daiwa_novatek.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-06
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Novatek disclosed upbeat 2Q26 results and 3Q26 guidance 
thanks to continued supply-chain cost pass-through, as well as share gain 
in the high-end smartphone OLED display driver (D/D) market. Though we 
raise forecasts and TP to reflect these unusual business dynamics amid 
the “silicon inflation”, we remain comfortable with our Hold (3) rating. That 
said, we are structurally positive on its leading position in design and 
technology for SoC diversification to cope with the D/D competition.

What’s the impact: Results and guidance all beat yet ... Novatek 
reported 2Q26 EPS of TWD10.4, beating our/consensus estimates by 
31%/36% thanks to robust gross margin (GM) that beat guidance (38-41%) 
and market expectation on supply-chain cost pass-through, favourable SoC 
mix and low-cost inventory, as well as frugal opex control and higher non-
operating gains associated with investment and dividend incomes (table on 
p.2). Guidance calls for 3Q26 revenue to still grow 5-9% QoQ regardless of 
a demand slowdown in the consumer space. Yet guided GM of 38.5-41.5% 
implies a QoQ drop due to no more low-cost inventory and an unfavourable 
mix as small-and-medium D/D will drive the growth, while SoC revenue will 
be flat and large D/D will drop QoQ, per management.

… business dynamics don’t seem to be structural. While our view 
calling for a demand rebalancing across TV and PC in the consumer space 
after customers’ order pull-in in 1H26 appears to be materialising, per 
management’s comments at today’s call, Novatek’s share gain in the high-
end smartphone OLED D/D market looks to be helping it offset the impact, 
with extra delta to be contributed by the continued pass-through of wafer 
and OSAT cost hikes through the supply chain. We therefore lift our 2026-
28E EPS by 2-21% but view these dynamics as unusual amid the “silicon 
inflation” environment. That said, we remain structurally positive on 
Novatek’s leading position in design capability and process technology for 
diversification into a broadened SoC portfolio to cope with any intense D/D 
competition, including machine-vision solutions for applications from 
surveillance to drones, smart wearables, smart appliances to robotics.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we raise our 12M TP 
for Novatek to TWD515 (previous: TWD430), still based on our 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 13x. We reaffirm our Hold (3) rating with key 
upside/downside risks of any further cost pass-through/deteriorated end-
demand on commodity price hikes.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 20-24% above the consensus, as 
we believe the street has yet to factor in the full effect of the cost pass-
through.

6 August 2026

Rebalancing still yet resilience on share gain

➢ 2Q26 results and 3Q26 guidance all upbeat  
➢ Consumer rebalancing offset by high-end smartphone share gain 
➢ Reiterating Hold (3) rating with a higher 12M TP of TWD515

Company profile 
Founded in 1997, Novatek is a fabless IC-design company. Its product range encompasses LCD-driver 
and control ICs, as well as video display, commercial and IM products.

## Document 089: 260806_daiwa_nypcb

Source PDF: all report pdf/260806_daiwa_nypcb.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-06
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: NYPCB released its 2Q26 results after market hours on 6 August. The earnings 
of TWD2.26bn (EPS: TWD3.50) are slightly lower than our expectation on a higher tax rate 
(24% vs. 18% in 1Q26). That said, core business was on track with our expectations. 
Looking ahead, we expect NYPCB to continuously increase its ASP (especially for BT 
substrate), which would lead to both revenue increase and margin expansion in 2H26. We 
currently forecast c.20%/10% revenue QoQ growth in 3Q26/4Q26 with gross margins 
expanding to c.30/35%. Separately, NYPCB also announced it will invest TWD46.8bn to 
construct a high body size and high layer count of substrate, which we believe to be on-line 
from 2029. We are currently reviewing our model and would provide updates in due 
course. We have a Buy (1) rating on NYPCB, with a 12-month TP of TWD2,444, based on 
a target PER of 49x, applied on 1-year-forward EPS.

What’s the impact: 
Below are our read-throughs from NYPCB’s 2Q26 results (released on 6 August).

• 2Q26 results first-take: results beat on pull-in demand. NYPCB released its results 
after market hours on 6 Aug, with net income of TWD2.26bn (EPS: TWD3.50), missing 
our expectations by 7% due mainly to a higher-than-expected tax rate. 2Q26 sales 
(TWD13.6bn; +21% QoQ; +42% YoY) was largely on track with our and street’s 
expectations due to price hikes. As such, gross margin also improved by 8.9pp QoQ to 
24.7% in 2Q26 (largely on-track with our estimate of 24.3%). With slightly better 
operating leverage, operating margin improved by 9.3pp QoQ to 21.4%. That said, net 
profit was dragged by a higher tax rate of 24% (vs. 18% in 1Q26).

• Business outlook. Looking ahead, we remain positive on the substrate industry given 
the  current S-D situation. We expect NYPCB to continuously increase its ASP 
(especially for BT substrate), which would lead to both revenue increase and margin 
expansion in 2H26. We currently forecast c.20%/10% revenue QoQ growth in 
3Q26/4Q26 with gross margins expanding to c.30/35%. Separately, NYPCB also 
announced it will invest TWD46.8bn to construct a high body size and high layer count 
of substrate, which we believe to be on-line from 2029. We are currently reviewing our 
model and would provide updates in due course.

### Page 1

M
Update
Acer Inc.  | Asia Pacific
2Q26 OpM missed our estimate
but was in line with the Street;
stay UW
2Q details:
• GM was 11.4% (+20bps q/q, +130bps y/y), slightly (10bps) below our
estimate, but 20bps above the Street. The improvement likely came from
better pricing power, which we mentioned in our Lenovo report.
• Opex of NT$7.3bn, higher than our NT$6.6bn estimate.
• Thus, OP was NT$1,783mn (+122% q/q, +143% y/y), 14% below our estimate,
but in line with the Street. OpM was 2.1% (+100bps q/q, +100bps y/y), 70bps

below our estimate, but in line with the Street.
• However, non-op income of NT$2,107mn was higher than our estimate/the
Street,  consisting mainly of FX gains (NT$332mn) and investment income
(NT$377mn).
• Therefore, NP  was NT$2,172mn (+209% q/q, +100% y/y), 49% above our
estimate and 51% above the Street, for EPS of NT$0.72.
Stay UW: We have turned more positive on OEMs' pricing power, but Acer remains
the OEM that has low-end and consumer exposure, making it harder to be defensive

on units and price, relative  to OEMs with higher commercial/enterprise exposure.
We view  CY27 P/E of 19x  as unattractive, compared to regional peers like Lenovo
(rated OW) at <12x F28e P/E.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lenovo (0992.HK)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.3% (based
on a beta of 1.1, risk-free rate of 2.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Stronger PC demand recovery in China
n Better cost savings and improved product mix

n Lower-than-expected increase in memory price
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Weaker PC demand recovery in China
n Deterioration in cost savings and product mix
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory price
Acer Inc. (2353.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We use RI model for Acer's valuation. Key assumptions
include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 0.9, equity risk premium of 10.2% and risk-free rate

of 0.5%), a 6.0% medium-term growth rate, and a 3% terminal growth rate.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macro recovery than expected
n Share gains in the PC market
n Better-than-expected cost controls
n Lower-than-expected increase in memory cost
Risks to Downside
n Weaker macro recovery than expected
n Share loss in the PC market
n Worse-than-expected cost controls
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory cost

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 100: 260806_ms_apmemory

Source PDF: all report pdf/260806_ms_apmemory.pdf

### Page 1

M
Idea
Remain OW and move to Top Pick:  We remain confident in S-SiCap, which is in
structural supply shortage (link), and VHM opportunities helped by edge AI (link).
Our new price target implies 2027e P/E of 47x and 2028e P/E of 26x; the 2028e P/
E    is roughly in line with the historical average since 2020.
2Q26 earnings beat: See Results recap                 .
Business update and outlook:
•  IoTRAM: 2Q revenue was NT$1.69bn, helped by stronger demand from

display customers. Management expects strong demand momentum, as
some customers shift from commodity memory to IoTRAM due to elevated
pricing. IoTRAM pricing was also increased in view of rising costs.
• S-SiCap: 2Q revenue was NT$640mn, as multiple cases have entered mass
production. IPD is entering an unprecedented rapid mass production ramp
supporting new embedded-in-substrate application for AI/HPC, with demand
visibility into 2028.  IPC production is set to resume in 4Q after potential

disruption in 3Q from the sale of PSMC's Tongluo fab. LSC has also entered
stable mass production. S-SiCap's revenue may exceed IoTRAM's in 2027.
• VHM: 2Q revenue was NT$37mn, mainly NRE. VHM is expected to enter
mass production in 2028. The first 8-high VHM is going through
qualification, with mass production expected around the end of 2027/ early
2028.
Other takeaways: AP Memory has entered  long-term capacity agreements with
major foundry and OSAT partners to secure wafer supply and OSAT capacity, and

expects to pay  deposits or prepayments to the partners. A GDR issuance has been
proposed, of up to 15mn shares (no more than 8.43% dilution),  for working capital
needs as the company sees strong growth opportunities.

Idea
2
Results recap
2Q revenue was NT$2.4bn, +13% Q/Q, +78% Y/Y, 5% above our estimate, driven by
IoTRAM and S-SiCap.
2Q IoTRAM/S-SiCap/VHM revenue mix was 71%/27%/2%.
2Q gross profit was NT$1.2bn, GM was 49.1%, +2.9ppt Q/Q, +6.8ppt Y/Y, 0.6ppt above our
estimate, helped by a more favorable customer mix.
2Q EPS was NT$4.18, +1% Q/Q, 5% above our estimate. Non-operating P&L included
mainly FX losses, interest income, and revaluation of financial assets.

Exhibit 1: AP Memory – 2Q results recap
Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research

### Page 3

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Estimate revisions summary
We  raise our EPS estimates  3% for 2027 and 7% for 2028: We factor in 2Q26 actual
results. We also raise our shipment estimates for IoTRAM products  to factor in the
spillover demand from commodity memory as commodity memory pricing remains
elevated. We assume a higher growth rate for S-SiCap to account for the progress the
company has made in volume ramp-up and strong guidance from  management.

Valuation methodology
We raise our price target to NT$1,666 from NT$1,555: We factor in our earnings
estimate changes.
Our residual income  model assumptions are unchanged, including:  1) cost of equity at
9.2% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.2 beta); 2) payout ratio of 67%; 3)
intermediate growth rate of 16.0%; and 4) terminal growth rate of 3%. Our bull and bear
case values rise to NT$1,966 (from NT$1,830) and NT$666 (from NT$620), respectively.

Risk Reward - AP Memory Technology Corp (6531.TW)
NT$1,666.00
Base case, derived from a residual income model. Key RI model assumptions include 1) cost ofequity constant at 9.2% (2.0% risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.2 beta); 2) medium-termgrowth rate of 16.0%, 3) payout ratio of 67%, 4) terminal growth rate of 3.0%.
MS Rating
▪S-SiCap revenue is likely to ramp in 2026-27, and be further driven by new IPDinterposer opportunities into 2028.▪Memory bandwidth and powerconsumption are the next issues to beresolved in AI computing, and we believe APMemory's wafer-on wafer (WoW) packagingtechnology could be an effective solution.▪The company expects memory business tobe stable in the near term.▪Overall AI business should continue to risein the mix – a positive read for the marginproﬁle.▪Our price target implies 47x 2027e P/Eand 26x 2028e P/E. 2028e P/E is roughly inline with the historical average since 2020.

10
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Macronix International Co Ltd (2337.TW)
Base case, P/B, in line with our Greater China memory coverage, due to the memory industry's
cyclical nature. We apply a target multiple of 7.0x to our 2026 BVPS estimate, vs. its historical
average of 1.5x since 2017. We continue to believe the P/B target is justifiable, as The MLC and
legacy TLC NAND could be in greater shortage into 2H26, with under supply easily going up

to 40%. The only supplier which could fill the gap remains Macronix.
Risks to Upside
n Wider legacy NAND supply / demand gap
n NOR up-cycle, driven by limited supply expansion and/or stronger demand
n Faster growth for Switch hardware and core titles
Risks to Downside
n Less severe legacy NAND supply / demand gap
n NOR down-cycle, driven by faster supply expansion and/or demand weakness
n Slower growth for Switch hardware and core titles

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 108: 260806_ubs_accton

Source PDF: all report pdf/260806_ubs_accton.pdf
Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

ab 6 August 2026
Global Research
First Read
Accton Technology Corporation
Trianium 3 and 800G  ramp driving H226 growth
Q226 ahead on better sales
Accton reported Q226 EPS of NT$19.78, ahead of UBS/Consensus estimates on stronger
topline growth and better margins. Q226 sales of NT$95.5bn was up 36% QoQ, ahead
of its original guidance of ~20% QoQ primarily driven by stronger pull in for 800G cloud
switch for its new social media customer and  faster ramp of Trainium 3 AI card. Q226

GM of 19.7% was up 0.2ppts QoQ on better product mix  and greater revenue scale,
resulting in OPM of 15.0% (+0.7ppts QoQ) on operating leverage (OPEX  grew 23%
QoQ including bonus accrual). We estimate data center switch accounted for 36% of
Q226 sales (vs 31% Q126), while accelerator card was 50% of total sales (53% Q126).
Q326 sales should continue to expand, despite component supply constraint
Accton expects Q326 sales to   improve QoQ with July sales reported at flattish MoM,

suggesting  25%+ QoQ growth,  above UBSe/consensus expectations. Management
noted the robust sale momentum  is supported by the continued ramp of Trainium 3 and
800G cloud switch deployment by its major customer and new social media customer.
Nevertheless, Accton noted it continued to see component supply constraint (ASIC IC,
PCB, MLCC, memory, etc.), which is capping its  near-term order fulfilment. We forecast
Accton's Q326 sales to grow 30% QoQ, while GM may arrive at 19.4% (-0.3ppt QoQ)

on mix change (AI card has lower GM), leading to OPM of 15.2% (+0.2ppt QoQ).
Increase value-add and new project ramps supporting 2027E momentum
We see multiple drivers supporting Accton to further increase its value-adds into 2027E,
on top of better shipments for AI cards and cloud switch businesses. We expect its ASP
for Trainium 3 to further increase from Q426-2027E, driven by the adoption of liquid
cooling in selective designs. We believe Accton's content in Trainium 4 (pilot in Q427)

should further increase on more complicated board design, further adoption of liquid
cooling, and potential CPO/NPO adoption. For the cloud switch business, we believe it is
working with its social media customer for ramping Nvidia's Spectrum 6 switch and
could potentially start to see revenue contribution from 2027E, while the ongoing speed
migration (800G/1.6T), supplying of switch trays, and introduction of OCS (Optical
Circuit Switch) will further increase Accton's content value. We also expect L11 server

rack  to see more meaningful sales contribution in 2027, versus minimal 2026.
Valuation: Reiterate Buy with NT$3,688 PT
We raise our 2026-28E EPS by +10%/2%/4% on stronger sales outlook for AI card /
cloud switch and better margins. We retain Buy rating and NT$3,688 PT, based on 26x
average 2027-28E PE (from 27x) on market valuation contraction, still in the upper half
of its historical 10-31x range and supported by a 52% EPS CAGR in 2025-28E. Stock has

pulled back 14% from recent peak and is trading at 20.7x 2027E PE. We believe the
stock should further re-rate on accelerating EPS growth from H226, Nvidia  CPO switch
ramp for new customer, and  potential opportunities in OCS.

Company Description
Founded in 1988, Accton specialises in ODM/OEM solutions for data center switches, AI
accelerator cards, enterprise/ODM switches, carrier access/telecom and others. Accton is one
of the largest vendors of white-box and OCP-compliant switches globally (by sales).
Leveraging deep expertise in high-speed switching, system integration and rapid time-to-

market, it has secured long-term partnerships with major cloud service providers.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Accton using PE methodology.
Key risks include: 1) weaker-than-expected demand for data center switches; 2) slower ramp
of 800G and 1.6T data center switches; 3) market share loss in the AI accelerator card
business; and 4) softer gross margin than expected.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.
Important Information About Sustainable Investing Strategies: Sustainable investing strategies aim to consider and incorporate environmental, social and governance
(ESG) factors into investment process and portfolio construction. Strategies across geographies approach ESG analysis and incorporate the findings in a variety of ways.
Incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing considerations may inhibit the portfolio manager’s ability to participate in certain investment opportunities that otherwise

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

## Document 110: 260806_ubs_aspeed

### Page 1

ab 6 August 2026
Global Research
ASPEED Technology Inc
AI winner story unchanged; bright outlook in
2027
Strong Q2 GM driven by price increase; Q3 to beat guidance
Aspeed reported Q2 GM of 72.0%, above consensus and UBSe of 69.3%/69.4%. We
believe this is mainly from the April price adjustment. Looking ahead, we estimate Q3
GM at 69.4%, slightly above guidance of 67-68%. With resilient Cloud AI demand and
improving supply of BT substrate and backend into H226, we forecast Aspeed's Q3 sales

to be NT$4.49bn, up 16% QoQ , outperforming the company guidance of NT$4.1-
4.3bn. For the entire year, we now estimate Aspeed to ship 31.0m BMCs (or up 63%
YoY) in 2026E, up slightly from our prior estimate of 30.8m. For 2027, we see further
growth potential for Aspeed's BMC shipment to reach 44.6m BMCs, a 44% YoY
growth, underpinned by CPU, GPU and ASIC servers across the US and China.
Initial 2027 orders tracking healthily with better product mix

Looking ahead into 2027E, we think the supply situation should further improve for
Aspeed. 1) Most of AST2700 shipment in 2027 should be using E-glass based BT
substrate, and 2) second qualified OSAT partner to enable steadier capacity support for
growing demand across both AI and general servers. Product mix wise, AST2700's mix is
on track to pick up meaningfully in 2027E, and should reach 26% of total shipment for
the year, generating a better blended ASP profile with AST2700's US$22-25 pricing,

above that of AST2600's cUS$16. As for the latest product developments: 1) the new
SMC 1840 with integrated FPGA is on track to start meaningful shipment by year-end
2027E and will contribute to sales in 2028E. 2) The new standalone SMC with Caliptra
integrated, the AST1040, with higher ASP (around US$10 vs current product of US$6.5-
7) should be shipping from late 2026E to improve SMC ASP in 2027E.
Strength across AI and general server unchallenged

As we have elaborated in our prior report, structurally, we continue to view Aspeed as a
key beneficiary of the rising agentic AI and server CPU opportunity. We forecast BMC
unit TAM for compute board to reach 77m in 2030E from 21m in 2025, with a further
10%+ upside risk from switches and storages.  See Figure 1S e r v e r s '  B M C  T A M  a n a l y s i s for our TAM analysis. Into
2027, the company should  solidify its lead across its current customer base, and we

believe Nvidia's Rubin Ultra is on track to use the 2700 series BMCs, signaling better
blended product and pricing structure of BMCs for GPU servers, on top of growth from
general and ASIC servers.
Valuation: Reiterate Buy and maintain our price target of NT$22,000
We raise net profit estimates by 4%/9%/11% for 2026E/27E/28E , respectively.
However, our EPS estimates for 2026E and beyond are largely unchanged, after
factoring in the 10% higher share count effective this quarter. Our price target is based

on a 55x average 2027-28E PE and a long-term earnings CAGR of 34%.

Company Description
ASPEED is the world's largest baseboard management controller (BMC) chipset provider.
ASPEED introduced the first generation of server management processors in 2005. It acquired
Emulex Pilot's remote server management chip business in 2016. Besides server management

chipsets, ASPEED also produces PC/AV extension solutions and image processing system-on-
chips (SoCs). In 2023, server management chipsets accounted for 88% of total sales.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We derive our price target for Aspeed from PE valuation methodology relative to its historical
trading range.
We believe key upside risks for Aspeed include: 1) stronger-than-expected server demand,
particularly from hyperscalers and from AI servers with less crowding out of traditional

servers; 2) further market share expansion; 3) faster-than-expected server platform upgrades;
4) faster-than-expected progress in expanding the addressable content within servers.
We believe key downside risks for Aspeed include: 1) weaker-than-expected server demand,
particularly from hyperscalers; 2) market share losses or more intense industry competition; 3)
slower-than-expected server platform upgrades; 4) slower-than-expected progress in
expanding the addressable content within servers.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

## Document 116: 260807_daiwa_sunon

Source PDF: all report pdf/260807_daiwa_sunon.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-07
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: Sunon posted its 2Q26 results today (7 August). We reaffirm 
our Buy (1) call as we expect the outlook for its telecom, servers, and 
industrial segments in 2026 to remain intact with both gross and operating 
margin set to expand.

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results largely in line with estimates; record-
high monthly revenue in July. Sunon’s 2Q26 revenue of TWD5,419m 
(+8.0% QoQ and +16.2% YoY) was in line with our and the consensus 
estimates. Its 2Q26 gross margin came in at 31.3% (vs. 31.7% in 1Q26 and 
30.5% in 2Q25), slightly missing our and the consensus estimates of 31.8% 
due to rising raw material costs. Therefore, its operating profit of TWD836m 
(+6.1% QoQ and +16.9% YoY) missed our and the consensus estimates by 
6.4% and 6.7%, respectively, after factoring in higher-than-expected admin. 
expenses. Its operating margin of 15.4% (vs. 15.7% in 1Q26 and 15.3% in 
2Q25) also missed our estimate of 16.6% and the consensus estimate of 
16.5%. With some non-op gains, its net profit of TWD690m (+6.3% QoQ 
and +84.5% YoY) was largely in line with our and the consensus estimates. 
Its 2Q26 EPS finished at TWD2.42, also largely in line with our (TWD2.39) 
estimate and the consensus (TWD2.49) estimates. On 5 August, Sunon 
reported a record-high monthly revenue for July of TWD2,119m (+14.2% 
QoQ, +31.7% YoY; +20.3% YoY for 7M26), accounting for 37.8% and 
36.4% of our previous and the consensus estimates, mainly due to the 
shipments of ASIC projects.

Solid revenue momentum likely to continue into 2H26. For 2H26, we 
expect its revenue to grow QoQ in light of ASIC projects that are expected 
to be launched. For its liquid cooling revenue, Sunon expects a single-
digit % revenue contribution in 2026, and intends to tap into enterprise, 
GPU, and ASIC clients. We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 2-5%, mainly on 
higher revenue assumptions.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD205 (from TWD200), now based on a target PER of 17x applied to 
our 1-year forward EPS forecast. Still around the midpoint of its past-5-year 
range of 7-25x, the target PER is slightly lower than 18x previously as we 
now forecast a net profit CAGR of 17.2% (previously 18.1%) over 2026-
28E (1x PEG). Downside risks: worse-than-expected gross-margin 
expansion and AI server development progress.

How we differ: We are one of only two overseas brokers covering the 
stock. Our 2028E EPS is 2% above the Bloomberg consensus, likely due to 
our higher revenue estimate.

7 August 2026

2Q26 results review: largely in line with estimates

➢ 2Q26 net profit in line with estimates; July revenue ahead of estimates 
➢ Strong 2026 outlook, driven by telecom, server, and industrial 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) call; raising our 12-month TP to TWD205

Company profile 
Founded in 1980 and listed on Taiwan’s main board in 2000, Sunonwealth Electric Machine Industry 
(Sunon) is one of the top-4 fan and motor providers in the world, along with Delta Electronics, Nidec and 
ebm-papst. Its major clients are Apple, Dell, Lenovo, Bosch, Siemens and Tesla. Its applications include 
IT & OE, telecoms & servers, industrial & medical equipment, home applications, distribution, autos and 
LED lighting.

## Document 120: 260807_ms_kingslide

Source PDF: all report pdf/260807_ms_kingslide.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-07
Pages: 16
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

M
Idea
Is the revenue growth momentum sustainable? Yes:  King Slide posted solid
monthly sales of NT$6,407mn (+44% MoM/+356% YoY) in July. To put this into
perspective, this monthly run rate was 3.3x vs. that in March 2026 (NT$1,918mn,
+9% MoM/+39% YoY). Though management did not provide numerical guidance on
the call, it indicates that it is seeing broad-based strong customer demand  with good
order visibility across a wide range of applications in both data center and enterprise

environments. Because of its comprehensive patent portfolio and solid execution,
King Slide  should remain the go-to supplier in most relevant projects. Therefore, we
now expect revenue to  step up further in 3Q26, to NT$19,555mn (+81% QoQ/+348%
YoY).
What's the margin outlook? Confident: The 87.4% GM (+9.7ppt QoQ) for 2Q26
was remarkable. Management cited a more favorable product mix – it shipped
plenty of new products, which usually carry higher margins thanks to more

complicated design. Management believes that  rail kit technology will continue to
advance –  and King Slide is able to better address those needs through accumulated
knowledge to efficiently move  design concepts into manufacturable products.
Furthermore, the product life cycle has shortened to 6-12 months from 12-24
months in the past, which will pose more challenges for the supply chain – but at
the same time, position leading players like King Slide well to offer more  value. As a

result, management sounds confident about its margin outlook in coming years.
Does King Slide have enough capacity? It's coming: This is another natural
question for a company showing such robust growth in a short period. Management
indicated that it has been squeezing more output from its existing facilities through
equipment/automation upgrades and more work shifts; utilization is 70-80%  now.
Aside from existing plants, it will have US capacity coming online this September/

October and a new factory in Kaohsiung in 2H27. Last, it might kick off another
project in late 2026 in Kaohsiung next to its existing one that could accommodate a
greenfield factory – potentially tripling its existing capacity when fully operational.
Stay OW: We maintain the view that King Slide's competitiveness is well entrenched
and accentuated amid quick generation migration. The stock has rallied very
strongly, yet we believe the earnings outlook can sustain valuation.

What’s Changed
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW) From To
Price Target NT$10,000.00 NT$15,000.00
King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Rome Wasn't Built in a Day; Lift
Price Target to NT$15,000
Management indicates that its industry position and GM profile
have been achieved gradually and should be sustained. We raise
our 2027 EPS estimate 53% and continue to apply a target P/E
of 30x, implying a PEG of 0.4. Stay OW.

Valuation Methodology
Our price target (base case scenario value) is NT$15,000 (previously NT$10,000):  It is
derived from our  target P/E of 30x 2027e (unchanged).
We use P/E as our valuation methodology because we believe it better captures King
Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our 30x target P/E multiple is justified by an
operating profit CAGR of 77% for 2025-28 in our estimates, implying a PEG of 0.4 vs. the
average of 1.1 over 2010-25, when the stock traded at 18x P/E  with an operating profit

CAGR of 18%.
Bull case scenario value – NT$20,000 (vs. NT$13,000 previously): We apply a 40x
multiple to our base case estimate for 2027 (unchanged). Our bull case mainly reflects
stronger demand for server rail kits owing to heavy server capex and spec upgrades driven
by the secular AI trend.
Bear case scenario value – NT$7,500 (vs. NT$5,000 previously): We apply a 15x
multiple to our base case estimate for 2027 (unchanged). Our bear case mainly reflects

lukewarm data center demand amid softening IT capex budgets and pricing pressure for
server components without spec upgrades.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 1

M
Update
Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
GB200 and GB300 NVL72
Racks in July 2026
We provide monthly/quarterly rack shipment forecasts for
ODMs. Within the major Server ODMs, our order of preference
is Wistron  > Hon Hai > Quanta, based on upside to PT.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Howard Kao
Equity Analyst
Howard.Kao@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2989
Irene Yen
Research Associate

Irene.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2869
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Andy Meng, CFA
Equity Analyst
Andy.Meng@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7689
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
Our CY26 full-year forecast is now at 80-85K racks: This bakes in our recently
raised ~3.8K rack-equivalent forecast at Wistron (report here), to ~17.9K racks. This

year is a strong one for downstream rack assembly. We expect over 100% y/y
growth in rack shipments, vs. ~29k last year.
Quanta (2382.TW, OW): July revenue was NT$366.3B (-5% m/m, +131% y/y). We
attribute the m/m decline mainly to lower NB shipments, at 2.8M units (-38% m/m).
As for GB200/300 rack shipments in the month, we estimate Quanta delivered 2.2-
2.25K racks (-4% m/m) in July. Our full-year assumption is unchanged, at ~18.7K
racks.
Wistron (3231.TW, OW): July revenue was  NT$308.2B (-4% m/m, +61% y/y). We

attribute the m/m decline to: 1) lower NB shipment, at 1.6M units (-20% m/m); 2)
lower DT shipments, at 700K units (-13% m/m); 3) lower monitor shipments, at
900K units (-10% m/m); and 4) slightly lower GB200/300 server computing tray
shipments (1,150-1,200 rack equivalents, by our estimates, down 6% m/m), offset by
higher Wiwynn revenue, at NT$117.7B (+6% m/m, +39% y/y). Our full-year forecast
was raised to ~17.9K racks recently.
Hon Hai (2317.TW, OW): July revenue was NT$946.5B (+15% m/m, +54% y/y), with

all segments growing m/m, with server-related revenue seeing the strongest m/m
growth, followed by computing products, followed by components and other
products, and lastly the consumer segment, which mainly includes iPhone assembly.
We model HH's GB rack shipments at 3.6K racks (+9% m/m). Our full-year forecast
is unchanged, at ~31.7K racks.

We estimate GB200/300 rack output in July at ~8.0K (flattish m/m), within

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
Base case scenario value, derived from a residual income valuation model. Our key assump-
tions include a cost of equity of 8.5%, a medium-term growth rate of 13%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Faster progress in AI server business
n Faster EV business development progress
n Any new M&A activity that could improve sentiment

Risks to Downside
n Lower iPhone sell-through
n Smaller profit contribution from AI business
n Slower EV business development progress
n Geopolitical developments that could negatively affect foreign investment
Quanta Computer Inc. (2382.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.0% (beta of
1.2, equity premium of 6.0% and risk-free rate of 1.5%), an 8.5% medium-term growth rate, and
a 3% terminal growth rate.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Stronger-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Stronger-than-expected server demand
n Faster-than-expected AI server penetration
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Softer-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Weak margin performance owing to rising labor costs and sales shortfalls
n Fierce price competition in the mega data center segment
n Slower-than-expected AI server penetration

Wistron Corporation (3231.TW)
Base case, residual income valuation. Key assumptions: 8.7% cost of equity, 7.0% medium-
term growth rate and 3% terminal growth rate.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected divestiture of consumer electronics business
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Margin expansion from better product mix

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

FOCI Fiber Optic Communications Inc  | Asia Pacific
We Expect 2Q Earnings to Mark
the Near-Term Trough – Stay
OW
2Q EPS came in better than feared:
• FOCI reported a loss of NT$0.23, narrowing from a NT$0.98 loss in 1Q and
beating our estimate of a NT$0.35 loss.
• Revenue rose 12% Q/Q but fell 27% Y/Y, 7% above our estimate yet 1% below
consensus.
• GM slipped Q/Q to 0.2%, reflecting destructive testing impact for its FAU
ahead of mass production and overhead from the 24-hour automation FAU

line.
• OpM improved to -21.4% from -40.9% in 1Q. 1Q was affected by one-off
share issuance expense.
July sales were NT$171mn, +9% M/M and +12% Y/Y – mainly driven by SiPh NRE
revenue.

Key Takeaways
CPO revenue should start in 3Q, subject to the customer’s final schedule.
The new fab in Thailand, in charge of traditional business manufacturing, has

begun equipment move-in, with mass production starting in September.
We expect a continued revenue climb from July into 2027, as more than one
customer enters mass production.
FOCI will host its on-site earnings call in early September. We expect more
details on CPO development and the expansion plan.
We view 27x 2027e P/E as undemanding.
